Algorithm for min-max matrix game:
- Maintain l over rows (distribution over constraints), b over cols.
- Row update (for max): l_k ∝ l_k exp(η (M b)_k) (rows with high payoff increase).
- Column update (for min): b_i ∝ b_i exp(-η (M^T l)_i) (cols with low weighted payoff decrease? Wait column minimizes max; against l, chooses min column. MW update for min player: b_i ∝ b_i exp(-η (M^T l)_i), since high columns increase cost, reduce.)

Fast LSE mirror descent implementation details:
- We'll represent vector a as float64 nonneg, normalized sum1.
- Function softmax_wrapped(z, T=None): z - z.max, exp, sum, return z/T? We need for weights over c and for b.
- Function get_weights(c, temperature): returns w normalized positive. Use stable: shifted=(c-cmax)/temperature; exp; sum; if underflow, okay with -inf? We can compute with T>1e-12.
- To keep weights not too sparse, set T_min maybe 1e-6*(cmax?)? But to approximate max, T small. Use adaptive T schedule: start T_high = 0.2*max_c? or 0.1? Since cmax~0.000625. T=0.01 times? Use relative temperature: T = rel_temp * cmax, where rel_temp e.g. 1.0 initially, then down to 0.01. If T=cmax, weights spread over all positive c; if T=0.01 cmax, only peak. This is scale invariant and stable. For LSE gradient, r = cross_weighted(a,w), where w sum1. r scale ~? w concentrated => r~a~1/n; if w spread over many k, r~1/n times number? Since w sum1, max term ~1. r between 0 and max a ~maybe 0.1/n? Actually w over c length 2n-1 sum1; each w≤1/K. r_i=∑_j a_j w_{i+j}, up to max a. So scale ~1/n. Good.
- Mirror step: a_new = a / (1 + alpha*r) or exp(-alpha*r). Since r max maybe ~0.01, alpha ~100 to get factor e^-1. Use relative alpha = target_decay / max(r, eps) (or q75). For larger T, r may be lower? Use q50.
- Accept if actual score improves or LSE improves. Maybe use LSE objective to guide: F_T(a)=logsumexp(c/T)/T? We can compute. Use actual score as final. Since T-schedule, use LSE for iteration, but accept based on actual score (true objective) to avoid degrading. However early large T may not immediately improve true score? But our objective likely decreases monotonically with small steps? LSE gradient of smooth approx not exact; may not monotonically decrease max. We can implement a generic local_loop(a, time_budget, T_schedule, step_target):
for T in schedule:
  for iter until time:
    c=fast_conv(a); current_score=...
    w=get_weights(c,T)
    r=cross_weighted(a,w)
    for alpha in set:
       a_try = a / (1+alpha*r)
       normalize
       score=eval
       if score < current_score: accept; break
    if accept count etc.

Let's think about local exact improvement using pair coordinate. If final score near 1.504, need fine. Coordinate descent may be essential. Let's design a robust "reduce peak" coordinate operation.

Given a, compute c and M, active peaks. We can try to reduce top peaks by moving mass from their top contributors to positions that do not increase other peaks. Pair swap: Choose p (indicator of high contribution to peak) and q (low). We want reduce M by ε. The derivative of M for ε = -2x_{p?} etc. We can find q such that derivative for active peaks is negative or small.

For a pair (p,q) with p reduced, q increased, change to c_k:
Δ_k = -2ε x_{k-p} + 2ε x_{k-q} - 2ε^2 * I(k=p+q). (Check if p+q=k gives -2ε^2? earlier coefficient -2. If p+q=k, cross-concave, helps reduce that peak beyond linear. Good.) So for active peak k, if k=p+q for top active, the quadratic term helps. Linear term is 2ε(x_{k-q}-x_{k-p}). To reduce active peaks, choose p with high x_{k-p} for active k and q with low x_{k-q} plus p+q active.
We can compute for each p (to reduce) the vector of contribution to active peaks: D_p = [x_{k-p}] for k active. For q (increase): E_q = [x_{k-q}], and bonus if p+q in active. We want E_q - a D_p ≤0 or small; and p+q active to get -ε^2. This is like finding low q relative to p. Since active peaks maybe few, we can score each q.

A greedy coordinate move:
- Determine active peaks A (top peak(s) or K peaks with c >= M - slack).
- For each i, compute D_i = sum over A of λ_k x_{k-i} (weighted) = r_i. High r_i means coordinate contributes to active peaks; reduce it.
- For each q, compute E_q = r_q? Actually r_q = ∑ λ_k x_{k-q}. If we move mass from p to q, first-order change in weighted sum ∑λ c_k = 2ε(r_q - r_p) -2ε^2 λ_{p+q} (if p+q in A). To reduce weighted sum, need r_q < r_p + (ε λ_{p+q}) maybe.
- Choose p largest r, q smallest r such that p+q in A? Then move small ε. This is exactly mirror step but pairwise and preserves sum without shrinking all. It should monotonically reduce weighted objective.
- We can perform exact line search for ε moving mass from p to q: objective max over all c_k, but we can evaluate actual score after moving. Since n=1200, evaluating one pair move is fast conv. We can try many candidate pairs and choose best small move.

This is promising for final polish: Use r from active weights; repeatedly pick top r and bottom r with bonus, perform a move with small ε (e.g. move 1% of x_p to q), evaluate. If score improves, accept; continue. This is like steepest descent with pairwise swaps. It can get very fine.

Computing c_new for candidate: We have current c and a, p,q, eps. Need compute max over L. We can construct delta array efficiently:
newc = c.copy()
# add -2eps * x_{k-p} for k where 0<=k-p<n: k=p..p+n-1 (bounded L-1)
newc[p:p+n] -= 2*eps*a[0:n] (since x_{k-p}=a[k-p] for k=p..p+n-1; p+n <=2n-1? p≤n-1, p+n-1≤2n-2 yes full length n)
# add +2eps * x_{k-q} for k=q..q+n-1: newc[q:q+n] += 2*eps*a
# subtract 2eps^2 at k=p+q if p+q < L: newc[p+q] -= 2eps^2

Mirror step from current c/w:

function pair_refine_active(a, time_budget):
  n, L; c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max(); best_score=...
  c_best = c.copy(); a_best = a.copy()
  no_improve=0; scan_count=0
  while time:
    # choose active peaks: maybe 1 to 5 top with c >= M*(1-1e-6) or top 5.
    top_idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
    # Maybe include all peaks with c >= M - 1e-6*M; ensure at least 1.
    A = top_idx[:K] where K = min(10, max(1, int(0.005*?)))? Use K=5.
    r = direct_r(a, A)
    # candidate p: top r among a>1e-10 (excluding maybe zeros)
    p_list = np.argsort(r)[::-1][:P]
    # q: bottom r among all (including maybe small), plus random
    q_list = np.argsort(r)[:Q]
    # shuffle? 
    best_candidate = None
    for p in p_list:
      if a[p] < 1e-12: continue
      for q in q_list:
        if q==p: continue
        for frac in frac_list:
          eps = frac*a[p]
          if eps <= 0: continue
          max_new = evaluate_pair_move_max(c,a,p,q,eps)
          if max_new < best_M - 1e-15:
             if best_candidate is None or max_new < best_candidate_max:
                best_candidate=(p,q,eps,max_new)
    if best_candidate:
       apply to a and c; M=best_candidate_max; best_score =2n*M; best_a=c? update; no_improve=0
    else:
       # Try active peaks top 1/2 maybe different K, or random p q
       if random tries fail and no_improve>50: perturb a (add small random noise) and recompute c; no_improve reset
    no_improve+=1
  return best_a

Now, details for `fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    size=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=rfft(a,size)
    c=irfft(A*A,size)[:L]
    # sym? convolution should be? np.convolve full not necessarily symmetric? Wait convolution of a with itself is not symmetric generally? Actually full convolution of a with itself has symmetry? Let's check a=[1,2,3,4]: conv=[1,4,10,16,18,16,10,4,12? wait np.convolve([1,2,3,4],[1,2,3,4])=[1,4,10,16,18,16,10,8,16]? Let's compute: [1,4,10,16,18,16,10,8,16]? That's not symmetric. Actually convolution of a with itself is symmetric if one is reversed? For autocorrelation yes; convolution not. Our c not necessarily symmetric. FFT A*A gives cyclic; full linear convolution via zero pad is correct, not force symmetric.
    return c
```
Wait earlier we assumed c_k = ∑ x_i x_{k-i} ordered pairs; this is convolution (not correlation), not symmetric unless x symmetric. FFT product A*A correct.

For cross_weighted r_i =∑ a_j w_{i+j}; uses product A * rfft(w_rev), correct.

But LSE weights w over convolution coefficients c_k. For derivative, we need derivative of c_k wrt a_i = 2a_{k-i} (ordered). The sum gives r_i via cross_weighted as above. Let's verify with pair c_k formula. Good.

Now, `lse_objgrad_u` with shared FFT:
```
def lse_value_grad_u(u, T):
   n=len(u)
   a=softmax_log(u)
   L=2*n-1
   size = 1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
   A = rfft(a, size)
   c = irfft(A*A, size)[:L]
   c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
   w, lse = softmax_logsumexp(c, T)
   w_rev = w[::-1]
   B = rfft(w_rev, size)
   h = irfft(A*B, size)
   r = h[L-1::-1][:n]
   g_a = 2.0 * r
   mean_g = np.dot(a, g_a)
   g_u = a * (g_a - mean_g)
   # actual value F = T*lse
   return T*lse, g_u, c, a
```
`g_u` should sum to ~0; can subtract mean_g? We used mean_g = dot(a,g_a), which is also (1/n?)? Weighted average with a, unnormalized because sum a=1. Good.

Potential issue: `size` for cross full length n+L-1=3n-2. For `A*A`, length 2n-1, same size okay. `irfft(A*B)` length size. Good.

`softmax_logsumexp` stable:
```
def softmax_logsumexp(z,T):
   m=np.max(z)
   z2=(z-m)/T
   e=np.exp(z2)
   s=e.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return np.ones(len(z))/len(z), m
   w=e/s
   return w, m/T + np.log(s)
```
If T extremely small and multiple max, e sum count; okay.

L-BFGS implementation details. Because objective in u has flat translation, we can keep u0 with maybe zeros? Use log a. When converting from a after accepted, use `u = np.log(np.maximum(a, tiny))`. The L-BFGS history should be reset between T stages. Within stage, if line search fails many times, break.
```
def lse_lbgfs(u0, T, time_limit, maxiter, verbose=False):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    n=len(u0)
    m_hist=20
    s=[]; y=[]; rho=[]
    f,g,_,a = lse_value_grad_u(u0,T)
    if not finite: ...
    best_f=f; best_u=u0.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_vec(a)
    it=0
    fails=0
    while it<maxiter and time.t...:
       it+=1
       # compute direction
       q=g.copy()
       alphas=[]
       for idx in range(len(s)-1,-1,-1):
           alpha = rho[idx]*np.dot(s[idx], q)
           alphas.insert(0, alpha) # or append and reverse later
           q = q - alpha*y[idx]
       r = q.copy()
       for idx,alpha in enumerate(alphas):
           beta = rho[idx]*np.dot(y[idx], r)
           r = r + (alpha-beta)*s[idx]
       d = -r
       d -= np.mean(d)
       # if dot(g,d) >=0, reset history and d=-g
       if np.dot(g,d) >= 0:
           s.clear();y.clear();rho.clear(); d=-g; d-=mean
       norm_d = np.linalg.norm(d)
       if norm_d < 1e-12: break
       # scale direction maybe to unit to aid backtracking:
       d = d / max(1, norm_d) ? If scale, dot g d changes. We can do but not necessary.
       step=1.0
       accepted=False
       for lr in range(25):
          trial = u + step*d
          f_new,g_new,_,a_new = lse_try...
          if finite and f_new <= f + 1e-4*step*np.dot(g,d) + 1e-12:
             accepted=True; break
          step*=0.5
       if not accepted:
          fails+=1
          if fails>5: break
          # clear history?
          s.clear();...
          continue
       fails=0
       # update history
       ds = step*d
       gy = g_new - g
       if np.dot(ds, gy) > 1e-14:
          s.append(ds); y.append(gy); rho.append(1/np.dot(ds,gy))
          if len(s)>m_hist: pop
       u=trial; f=f_new; g=g_new
       # track best actual score? We can compute score_vec(a_new) maybe every 10 or if f improved by 1e-8.
       if f < best_f:
          best_f=f; best_u=trial.copy(); best_a=a_new.copy(); # maybe actual
       if it%20==0: best_score = score_vec(a_new); if best_score < best_score: best_a=a_new.copy()
    return best_a, best_score
```
This might be slow due to `lst.value`? Use local variables. It may use too many function evals. We'll set maxiter based on expected time; but inside breaks if too many nonfinite.

```
def lse_continue(a, time_budget, verbose=False):
    n=len(a); t0=time.monotonic()
    a = normalize(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_vec(a)
    # stages rel
    stages=[2.0,1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.12,0.07,0.04,0.022,0.012]
    # maybe if time budget small use fewer tail
    # We'll allocate 15% time for first 3, then increasing? Use per_stage = time_budget/(len*1.3)
    u = log(a)
    for rel in stages:
       if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_budget*0.95: break
       T=rel/n
       time_stage = min(time_budget - (time.monotonic()-t0), time_budget/(len*0.8))
       maxiter = max(50, min(3000, int(1000 * time_stage / max(1,time_budget/10))))? Too complex.
       set maxiter=800 for n<=1200, 400 for n>1800.
       a, sc = lse_lbgfs(u, T, time_stage, maxiter)
       update best
       # if no improvement for a stage, perturb? We can add small noise: a = normalize(a + rng normal * 0.01)
       u = log(a)
    return best_a,best_score
```

Pair refine active implementation more concrete:
```
def active_r(a, A):
    n=len(a); r=np.zeros(n)
    for k in A:
        js = max(0, k-n+1)
        je = min(n-1, k)
        idx = np.arange(js, je+1)
        r[idx] += a[k-idx]
    if len(A)>1: r/=len(A)
    return r
```
This r is unweighted average; high if a coordinates contribute. Good.

Candidate evaluation:
```
def pair_move_max(c, a, p, q, eps):
    # returns max without modifying
    # affected range min(p,q) to max(p,q)+n-1 maybe
    # simpler copy
    newc = c.copy()
    newc[p:p+n] += -2.0*eps*a
    newc[q:q+n] += 2.0*eps*a
    if p+q < len(c): newc[p+q] -= 2.0*eps*eps
    return float(np.max(newc))
```
Since p:p+n length n? p can be n-1, p+n length 2n-1= L, okay (p+n = 2n-1, slice valid length n? For L=2n-1, start n-1, stop 2n-1 exclusive, length n yes). q similarly. Good.
But note linear term for shifting p: c_k' = c_k - 2ε a_{k-p} for k=p..p+n-1; adding +2ε a_{k-q}. Yes.
If p and q both affect same coefficient, copy handles.
 Numerical small negative max? Use max(newc). If newc dips negative, irrelevant.

Could optimize by precomputing `a` and using local variables.

```
c[p:p+n] -= 2*eps*a_old # careful a changed? Use old a in delta. If we apply to a after using old, c delta uses old x. Do delta first:
newc=c.copy(); ... using old a
c=newc; a[p]-=eps; a[q]+=eps; if a[p]<0 set 0 and adjust? eps <= a[p].
```

```
    u = u0 - np.mean(u0)
    f,g,_,a = lse_objgrad_u(u,T)
    best_f=f; best_u=u.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_vec(a)
    s_list=[]...
    it=0; fail_count=0
    while it<maxiter and time...:
       it+=1
       # build d
       q=g.copy(); alphas=[]
       for idx in reversed(range(len(s_list))):
          alpha = rho[idx]*np.dot(s_list[idx], q)
          alphas.append(alpha)
          q = q - alpha*y_list[idx]
       r=q.copy()
       for idx in range(len(alphas)-1,-1,-1): # since alphas reversed order? Need standard order.
          alpha = alphas[idx]?
```
Let's implement correctly without insert:
Algorithm:
```
alphas = [0]*m
for i in range(m-1,-1,-1):
    alphas[i] = rho[i]*np.dot(s[i], q)
    q -= alphas[i]*y[i]
r = q
for i in range(0,m):
    beta = rho[i]*np.dot(y[i], r)
    r += (alphas[i] - beta)*s[i]
d = -r
```

`lse_value_grad_u(u,T)`:
```
def lse_value_grad_u(u,T):
    n=len(u)
    a=softmax_log(u)
    L=2*n-1
    size=1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
    A=rfft(a,size)
    c=irfft(A*A,size)[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    w,lse=softmax_logsumexp(c,T)
    B=rfft(w[::-1],size)
    h=irfft(A*B,size)
    r=h[L-1::-1][:n]
    g_a=2.0*r
    mean_g=np.dot(a,g_a)
    g_u=a*(g_a-mean_g)
    # center u? no
    return T*lse, g_u, a, c
```
If h has tiny negative, r may negative; g_u maybe. r should nonnegative; set `r=np.maximum(r,0)`. Good.
Potential issue: `size = 1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()`: for n=1, 3n-2=1; (1-1).bit_length()=0? Actually (0).bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1. For n=1, L=1, size for cross=1. Good.
But `1 << (size_needed - 1).bit_length()` if size_needed is power of two? Example L=3, size_needed=3; (3-1)=2.bit_length=2; 1<<2=4 correct. If size_needed=4, (4-1)=3.bit_length=2; 1<<2=4 correct. Use helper `next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()`.

```
def lse_lbgfs(u0,T,time_budget,maxiter=2000):
   t0=time.monotonic(); m_h=10; ...
   f,g,a,c = lse_value_grad_u(u0,T)
   best_f=f; best_a=a.copy(); best_score=2n*max(c)
   it=0; fail=0
   while it<maxiter and time...:
       it+=1
       # direction ...
       d = -r; d -= mean
       if dot(g,d) >=0 or not finite:
           if first? clear hist; d=-g; d-=mean
       maxd=np.max(np.abs(d)); if maxd<1e-14: break
       d = d/maxd * 0.35
       gd = np.dot(g,d)
       if gd >=0:
          # If after scaling gd positive, try -gradient directly?
          d = -g; d-=mean; maxd=...; d=d/maxd*0.35; gd=np.dot(g,d)
          if gd>=0 or maxd==0: break
       step=1.0; accepted=False
       for _ in range(18):
           trial = u + step*d; trial -= np.mean(trial)
           f_trial,g_trial,a_trial,c_trial = lse_value_grad_u(trial,T)
           if finite:
               # Armijo or more decrease
               if f_trial <= f + 1e-4*step*gd + 1e-13 or f_trial < f - 1e-12:
                  accepted=True; break
           step*=0.5
       if not accepted:
           fail+=1
           if fail>4:
              s_hist.clear(); y_hist.clear(); rho.clear(); fail=0; continue
           if it>10: break? maybe clear and continue
           continue
       fail=0
       # update history
       ds=step*d; gy=g_trial-g
       if np.dot(ds,gy) > 1e-15:
          append, trim
       else: clear
       u=trial; f=f_trial; g=g_trial; a=a_trial
       sc=2*n*np.max(c_trial)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       if f < best_f - 1e-13: best_f=f; best_u=u.copy()
   return best_a,best_score
```

```
def lse_continue_from(a, time_budget, short=False, seed=None):
   t0=time.monotonic(); n=len(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_vec(best_a)
   if short:
       stages=[1.0,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.025]
       iter_factor=0.5
   else:
       stages=[1.2,0.7,0.4,0.22,0.12,0.07,0.04,0.022,0.012,0.006]
   n_stages=len(stages)
   # maybe if time budget small use fewer stages: choose based on budget
   if time_budget < 120: stages=stages[:3]
   for rep in range(2):
       # for rep1 only last half stages to refine
       start_idx = 0 if rep==0 else max(0,len(stages)-3)
       for idx in range(start_idx,len(stages)):
          rel=stages[idx]
          T=rel/n
          # time per stage: allocate proportion: more for later? Use rem/n_stages*1.5
          rem=deadline? pass time_budget? Inside local time. We'll use fraction of original budget with rep.
          time_stage = (time_budget/(n_stages*(1+rep))) * 1.6
          if time_stage < 2: continue
          u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); u-=np.mean(u)
          a_stage, sc = lse_lbgfs(u,T,min(time_stage, deadline? no), maxiter=20000)
          # update best
          if sc < best_score: ...
          # After each stage, if current a (returned best in stage) score maybe worse; set current to best_a? For continuation, use current stage best, not global, to allow path. We'll set a = normalize(a_stage) if its actual score <= best_score*1.05 else best_a.
          sc_a = score_vec(a_stage)
          if sc_a <= best_score * 1.1 + 1e-8:
              a = normalize(a_stage)
          else:
              a = best_a.copy()
          # optional small perturbation if no improvement
   return best_a,best_score
```

```
def pair_refine(a, time_budget, seed=123):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n=len(a); L=frac?; c=fast_conv(a); M=float(np.max(c)); best_score=2*n*M; best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy()

    no_improve=0; accepted_count=0; scan=0

    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget:
       # if drift, recompute c from a occasionally

          # recompute to be safe? Could be expensive but okay

       # choose active peaks

       # include maybe if second peak close more

       r = np.zeros(n)
       for k in A: ... r[idx]+=a[k-idx]

       order = np.argsort(r)[::-1]
       P = min(8, n)
       p_list=[]
       for idx in order[:P]:
           if a[idx] > 1e-12:
              p_list.append(int(idx))
           if len(p_list)>=4: break
       # q list bottom Q and random

       if len(low_indices)>0:

          c = newc
          a[best_p] -= best_eps; a[best_q] += best_eps
          if a[best_p] < 0: a[best_p]=0
          # normalize? Sum should preserve; but due to drift, renormalize
          a = normalize_positive(a)
          # recompute c after normalize? If normalize tiny, scale changes c by s^2; but sum preserved almost. To be safe if normalize factor not 1, recompute c or scale c.

          M = float(np.max(c)); accepted_count+=1
          if M < best_score/(2*n) - 1e-15: best_score=2*n*M; best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
          no_improve=0
       else:
```

```
def next_pow2(m):
```

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(max(1,n), dtype=np.float64)/max(1,n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 1e-15: return np.ones(n)/n
    return a/s
```
If input scalar? a=np.asarray scalar 0d, len error. Ensure at least 1d: `a = np.atleast_1d(...)`.

`softmax_log`:
```
def softmax_log(u):
    u=np.asarray(u,dtype=np.float64)
    m=np.max(u)
    e=np.exp(u-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return ones
    return e/s
```
If u length 0? no.

`softmax_logsumexp`: if z length 1, returns [1], m.
```
def softmax_logsumexp(z,T):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    m=float(np.max(z))
    if T <= 1e-300:
       idx=np.where(np.abs(z-m) <= 1e-12*max(1.0,abs(m))+1e-300)[0]
       w=np.zeros_like(z); w[idx]=1.0/len(idx); return w,m
    z2=(z-m)/T
    e=np.exp(z2)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0:
       w=np.zeros_like(z); w[z==m]=...; return w,m
    e=e/s; return e, m/T+np.log(s)
```
`z2` could underflow all except max; s >=1.

`fast_conv` use `np.real`? irfft returns real. Use `c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)`? no.

`score_vec`:
```
def score_vec(a):
   a=np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(a,float))
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=float(np.sum(a))
   if s<0.01: return inf
   n=len(a); c=fast_conv(a/s)? In given code if not normalized, sum s; score=2n max(conv(a,a))/s^2. Use that exact.
   c=fast_conv(a)
   return float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(s*s))
```
But for our normalized, using a directly. If a has large values, conv maybe large; okay. We may pass normalized.

`lse_value_grad_u`: Need if `T` too small relative to c range: softmax stable.
```
def lse_value_grad_u(u,T):
   n=len(u)
   a=softmax_log(u)
   L=1 if n==0? n>=1
   L=2*n-1
   size=next_pow2(L) for conv? Wait for cross need next_pow2(n+L-1)=next_pow2(3*n-2). Use size_cross for both.
   size=next_pow2(max(1,3*n-2))
   A=rfft(a,size)
   c=irfft(A*A,size)[:L]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   w,lse=softmax_logsumexp(c,T)
   B=rfft(w[::-1],size)
   h=irfft(A*B,size)
   r=h[L-1::-1][:n]
   r=np.maximum(r,0.0)
   g_a=2.0*r
   mean_g=float(np.dot(a,g_a))
   g_u=a*(g_a - mean_g)
   g_u -= np.mean(g_u) # ensure zero due to numerical
   val=T*lse
   return val, g_u, a, c
```
Is centering u after g? no.

`lse_lbgfs`: We need handle `time_budget` maybe 0. Return initial a.
```
def lse_lbgfs(u0, T, time_budget, maxiter=10000):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    u0 = np.asarray(u0,float).copy()
    u0 -= np.mean(u0)
    n=len(u)
    if time_budget <=0: return softmax_log(u0), score_vec(...)
    m_hist=12
    s_list=[]; y_list=[]; rho_list=[]
    f,g,a,c = lse_value_grad_u(u,T)
    if not finite f: ...
    best_score=2*n*float(np.max(c)); best_a=a.copy(); best_f=f
    it=0; fails=0
    while it<maxiter and time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget:
       it+=1
       # build direction
       Mh=len(s_list)
       q=g.copy(); alphas=np.zeros(Mh)
       for i in range(Mh-1,-1,-1):
          alphas[i]=rho_list[i]*np.dot(s_list[i], q)
          q = q - alphas[i]*y_list[i]
       r=q.copy()
       for i in range(Mh):
          beta=rho_list[i]*np.dot(y_list[i], r)
          r = r + (alphas[i]-beta)*s_list[i]
       d=-r
       d -= np.mean(d)
       gd=np.dot(g,d)
       if (not finite gd) or gd >= 0.0:
          s_list.clear(); y_list.clear(); rho_list.clear()
          d=-g; d-=np.mean(d); gd=np.dot(g,d)
       maxd=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       if not finite maxd or maxd <= 1e-14:
          break
       d = d / maxd * 0.35
       gd=np.dot(g,d)
       if gd >= 0.0 or not finite gd:
          d=-g; d-=np.mean(d); maxd=...; d=d/maxd*0.35; gd=dot
          if gd>=0: break
       step=1.0; accepted=False
       for ell in range(18):
           trial = u + step*d
           trial -= np.mean(trial)
           f_trial,g_trial,a_trial,c_trial = lse_value_grad_u(trial,T)
           if np.isfinite(f_trial):
                if f_trial <= f + 1e-4*step*gd + 1e-12 or f_trial < f - 1e-12:
                    accepted=True; break
           step *= 0.5
       if not accepted:
           fails +=1
           if fails > 5:
               # try a pure small gradient step maybe
               s_list.clear(); ...
               d=-g; d-=mean; norm=...; if norm>0:
                  trial = u - 0.05*d/norm? Actually if gradient descent; we can accept any decrease by trying scales.
               if still fail: break
           else:
               s_list.clear(); y_list.clear(); rho_list.clear()
           continue
       fails=0
       ds=step*d; gy=g_trial-g
       if np.dot(ds,gy) > 1e-14:
           s_list.append(ds); y_list.append(gy); rho_list.append(1.0/float(np.dot(ds,gy)))
           if len(s_list)>m_hist: s_list.pop(0); ...
       else:
           clear
       u=trial; f=f_trial; g=g_trial
       sc=float(2*n*np.max(c_trial))
       if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
           best_score=sc; best_a=a_trial.copy()
       if f < best_f - 1e-13:
           best_f=f; best_u=u.copy()
    return normalize_positive(best_a), best_score
```
Potential issue: `f_trial < f - 1e-12` absolute too strict if f ~1e-4 and improvements small 1e-15? But early improvements bigger. For near optimum, Armijo condition with gd negative gives `<= f + small negative tolerance`; if f_trial slightly less, accepted. If no improvement but configurable. If f not decreasing, line search may fail; okay.
Maybe use `rtol=1e-8` and `atol=1e-14`: `f_trial <= f*(1+1e-8) + 1e-14 + step*gd*1e-4`. If f positive, allow small. We can do:
```
if f_trial <= f + max(1e-12, 1e-8*abs(f)) or f_trial <= f + 1e-4*step*gd + 1e-13:
```
But if gd negative, second is below f; first allows increase. To prevent too much increase, use both? Standard Armijo is `f_trial <= f + c*gd`. Since f small, add atol `1e-12`. Good. We'll use `f_trial <= f + 1e-4*step*gd + 1e-12`. If f decreases by 1e-13 but tolerance 1e-12, accepted. Good. If gd = -1e-8, tolerance f-1e-12, okay.

Line search step scaling: We scale d max 0.35, step maybe 1. If f_trial not accepted and step halves many, eventually step 0.000... u change tiny; due to atol may accept even if not decreasing? `f + gd*step*1e-4 +1e-12`; if step tiny, condition f_trial <= f+1e-12, could accept a non-decrease up to 1e-12. def lse_continue_from(a, time_budget, levels=6, seed=0):
```
    t0=time.monotonic()

    n=len(a)

    if levels == 'long': stages=[1.2,0.7,0.4,0.22,0.12,0.07,0.04,0.022,0.012,0.006]
    elif ...

    # Maybe if n>2000 use fewer large stages
    if n > 2000: stages=stages[:6]
    reps = 2 if time_budget > 300 else 1
    for rep in range(reps):
       # for rep1 only lower half stages
       start = 0 if rep==0 else max(0, len(stages)-3)
       for idx in range(start, len(stages)):
          rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
          if rem < 2: break
          rel = stages[idx]
          T = rel / max(1,n)
          # allocate time: for first rep, 1/n_stages; for second, maybe 1/n_stages but less

          time_stage = max(1.0, min(rem, base_alloc))

          u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); u-=np.mean(u)
          a_stage, sc_stage = lse_lbgfs(u,T,time_stage, maxiter=5000)
          sc_a = sc_stage
          if sc_a < best_score - 1e-12:
              best_score=sc_a; best_a=normalize_positive(a_stage)
          # decide current a: if stage current not much worse than best, use it to continue; else use best

          else:
              a = best_a.copy()
          # optional add tiny noise if no improvement

              rng = np.random.default_rng(100+rep+idx)

    rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)

        T=0.004/n
        u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))-mean
        a_stage, sc_stage = lse_lbgfs(u,T,rem*0.9,maxiter=5000)
        if sc_stage < best_score: best...
    return best_a,best_score
```

`pair_refine` implementation details code carefully.

`pair_move_max`:
```
def pair_move_max(c, a, p, q, eps):
    n=len(a); L=len(c)
    newc = c.copy()
    scale = 2.0*eps
    newc[p:p+n] -= scale * a
    newc[q:q+n] += scale * a
    s = p+q
    if s < L:
        newc[s] -= scale*eps
    return float(np.max(newc))
```
If p:p+n goes beyond L? p max n-1, p+n=2n-1=L, okay. q similarly.
Could use `L` local.

`apply_pair_move`:
```
def apply_pair_move(c,a,p,q,eps):
    n=len(a); L=len(c); scale=2.0*eps
    c[p:p+n] -= scale*a
    c[q:q+n] += scale*a
    s=p+q
    if s<L: c[s] -= scale*eps
    a[p] -= eps
    a[q] += eps
    # fix tiny negative
    if a[p] < 0: a[p] = 0.0
    a[np.abs(a)<1e-300]=0? not needed
    return c,a
```
Since c modified in place. Use old a in delta before update? We use a in slices, then update a. Good.

In pair_refine, after applying, sum scale. `s = a.sum(); if not close: a/=s; c/=(s*s)`.
But if a[p] negative set to 0 after sum? The move should not negative. Floating can tiny negative; set before sum. If set, sum less than 1 by eps_tail; normalize.

But if r[p] and r[q] both similar due flat, pair still might improve due quadratic; but those are local stationary; random/noise needed.
We'll not skip based on r to ensure candidates; exact eval will filter. But to reduce, we can choose q list only low r.

`q_list` can include bottom by r; so r[q] <= r[p] usually.

`p_list`: top by r among a> threshold. If high r coordinates are many, top.

When no improvement, random pairs: choose p from top half r, q from bottom half. Try frac [0.05,0.15]. If none, perturbation.

Perturbation: `a = best_a * np.exp(noise * scale)` where scale maybe 0.05? If a ~1/n, noise multiplicative 5% can significantly change. Use scale 0.1. Then normalize. If score worse than best*1.05, maybe too much; try smaller scales [0.05,0.02,0.01] until not too bad, else stay best.

Pseudo final pair_refine:
```
def pair_refine(a, time_budget, seed=1):
    if time_budget < 1: return normalize_positive(a), score_vec(a)
    t0=...
    a=normalize_positive(a)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    c=fast_conv(a); M=float(np.max(c)); best_score=2*n*M; best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve=0; scans=0; accepted=0
    frac_list=(0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget:
        scans +=1
        # if c and a inconsistent? every 20 accepted recompute
        if accepted and accepted % 20 == 0:
            c=fast_conv(a); M=float(np.max(c))
            if M < best_score/(2*n) - 1e-14: best...
        stage = min(3, no_improve//25)
        P = [3,5,7,10][stage]
        Q = [15,25,40,55][stage]
        K = [3,4,5,6][stage]
        # active peaks
        if K >= L: top_idx = np.argsort(c)
        else: top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        # r
        r=np.zeros(n)
        for kk in top_idx:
           k=int(kk)
           js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)
           idx=np.arange(js,je+1)
           r[idx] += a[k-idx]
        r /= max(1,len(top_idx))
        # p list
        order=np.argsort(r)[::-1]
        p_list=[]
        for idx in order:
           idx=int(idx)
           if a[idx] > 1e-10:
              p_list.append(idx)
           if len(p_list) >= P: break
        # q list
        q_list=[]
        for idx in order[-Q:]:
           idx=int(idx)
           if idx not in q_set: q_list.append(idx) # use set
        # add a few random low
        low_cut = np.quantile(r, 0.25)
        low_idx = np.where(r <= low_cut)[0]
        if len(low_idx) > 0:
           add = rng.choice(low_idx, size=min(10,len(low_idx)), replace=False)
           for idx in add:
              idx=int(idx)
              if idx not in q_set: ...
        # evaluate
        best_mn=None; bp=bq=be=0
        for p in p_list:
           ap=a[p]
           if ap <= 0: continue
           for q in q_list:
              if q == p: continue
              # optional skip if q+ p active top? if p+q in top_idx and c[p+q] >= 0.9999*M: maybe continue
              for frac in frac_list:
                 eps=frac*ap
                 if eps <= 0: continue
                 # If eps is so small no improvement? min reduction maybe
                 mn = pair_move_max(c,a,p,q,eps)
                 if mn < M - 1e-15:
                    if best_mn is None or mn < best_mn:
                       best_mn=mn; bp=p; bq=q; be=eps
                    # break for this q? If larger frac maybe worse; smaller frac maybe lower? We can continue to find even lower; but if mn close to M, okay. For speed, if mn < M*(1 - 1e-5) maybe break? Keep simple continue.
        if best_mn is not None:
           c,a = apply_pair_move(c,a,bp,bq,be)
           s=a.sum()
           if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15: c=fast_conv(a); a=normalize_positive(a); M=float(np.max(c))
           else:
              if abs(s-1.0) > 1e-12:
                 a /= s; c /= (s*s)
              M = float(np.max(c))
           accepted += 1
           if M < best_score/(2*n) - 1e-15:
              best_score = 2*n*M; best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
           no_improve = 0
        else:
           improved=False
           pos_idx=np.where(a > 1e-9)[0]
           if len(pos_idx) > 1:
              low_cut=np.quantile(r,0.2)
              low_idx=np.where(r <= low_cut)[0]
              if len(low_idx)==0: low_idx=pos_idx
              for _ in range(30):
                 p=int(rng.choice(pos_idx)); q=int(rng.choice(low_idx))
                 for frac in (0.03,0.1,0.25):
                    eps=frac*a[p]
                    mn=pair_move_max(c,a,p,q,eps)
                    if mn < M - 1e-15:
                       c,a=apply_pair_move(...); s...; M...; improved=True; break
                 if improved: break
           if improved:
              accepted +=1
              if M < best_score/(2*n)-... update
              no_improve=0
           else:
              no_improve += 1
              if no_improve > 100:
                  # perturb from best
                  scales=(0.15,0.08,0.04,0.02)
                  a_cur=None
                  for sc in scales:
                     noise=rng.normal(size=n)*sc
                     a_try=best_a*np.exp(noise)
                     a_try=normalize_positive(a_try)
                     sc_try=score_vec(a_try)
                     if sc_try <= best_score*(1.0+0.02*sc)+1e-8:
                        a_cur=a_try; break
                  if a_cur is None:
                     a_cur=best_a.copy()
                  a=normalize_positive(a_cur); c=fast_conv(a); M=float(np.max(c)); no_improve=0; accepted=0
                  # if M too bad, revert next no_improve? We keep best.
              if no_improve > 800:
                  break
        if scans % 200 == 0:
            print(f"    pair_refine {time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s scans={scans} best={best_score:.8f}")
    # final recompute best
    final_a=np.maximum(best_a,0); fsum=final_a.sum(); if fsum>0: final_a/=fsum
    final_score=score_vec(final_a)
    if final_score < best_score: best_score=final_score; best_a=final_a
    return final_a,best_score
```

Now, structured_init and interpolation.
```
def interpolate_sequence(f,new_n):
   old_n=len(f); if old_n==new_n: return f.copy()
   f=normalize_positive(f)
   xs=np.linspace(0,1,old_n); xn=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   g=np.interp(xn,xs,f)
   return normalize_positive(g)
```
`structured_init(n, mode, rng)` returns normalized.
Modes:
0 cosine edge? cos(pi*x/2) decreasing from1 to0.
1 sine bell.
2 Gaussian center.
3 edges: exp(-8*x^2)+exp(-8*(x-1)^2)
4 linear skew: 1+0.6*(x-0.5)? hmm
5 oscillatory: 1+0.3*cos(4πx)+0.2*cos(8πx)
6 random smooth: with random freq.
Ensure nonneg; normalize; if sum zero uniform.

Let's implement `conv_ab` using FFT:
```
def conv_ab(a,b):
   n=len(a); m=len(b)
   L=n+m-1
   size=next_pow2(L)
   A=rfft(a,size); B=rfft(b,size)
   return irfft(A*B,size)[:L]
```
If a,b same length n. Good.
`cross_weighted(a,w)`: as earlier with size next_pow2(len(a)+len(w)-1).
Then selfplay:
```
def entropic_selfplay(a0, time_budget, seed=5):
   a = normalize_positive(a0)
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_vec(a)
   rng=...
   t0=...
   rel_temp=0.1
   block_iters=50
   outer=0
   while time...:
       b = a.copy()
       lam = np.ones(L)/L
       for it in range(block_iters):
           v = conv_ab(a,b)
           vmax = float(np.max(v))
           if vmax <=0: break
           T = rel_temp * vmax
           lam, _ = softmax_logsumexp(v, T)
           r = cross_weighted(a,lam)
           rmax = float(np.max(r))
           if rmax <=0: break
           Tc = rel_temp * rmax
           b_neg = -r
           b, _ = softmax_logsumexp(b_neg, Tc)  # This uses softmax of negative r; fine
       # now b approx best response
       sc_b = score_vec(b)
       if sc_b < best_score: best...
       # blends
       for t in (0.3,0.6,1.0):
           cand = (1-t)*a + t*b
           cand = normalize_positive(cand)
           sc=score_vec(cand)
           if sc < best_score: best...
           if sc < score_vec(a): a=cand.copy()
       if time...
       # recompute score a
       outer...
   return best_a,best_score
```
Using `softmax_logsumexp(-r,Tc)` gives b high where r low. Good.

`cross_weighted` implementation:
```
def cross_weighted(x,w):
    nx=len(x); nw=len(w)
    size=next_pow2(nx+nw-1)
    A=rfft(x,size); B=rfft(w[::-1],size)
    h=irfft(A*B,size)
    return h[nw-1::-1][:nx]
```
Since w length L. Good.

It modifies newc; if p and q ranges overlap, the order doesn't matter because we're adding two independent terms to same elements. We do subtract then add; okay. For p+q, subtract additional. Good.

Now, one subtle issue: Pair move formula for full convolution with ordered pairs uses `newc[k] += -2eps a[k-p] + 2eps a[k-q]`. This is correct when both p,q valid. But if p+q term: The linear terms for k=p+q include -2eps a[q] + 2eps a[p]; quadratic should be? Let's derive with ordered conv:
Original c_k = ∑_{i} a_i a_{k-i}. Change a_p to a_p-ε, a_q to a_q+ε. Terms affected:
- i=p: (a_p-ε) a_{k-p} = a_p a_{k-p} - ε a_{k-p}
- i=k-p (if !=p): a_{k-p} (a_p-ε) = a_{k-p}a_p - ε a_{k-p}; total -2ε a_{k-p}
- i=q: +ε a_{k-q}
- i=k-q: if !=q: +ε a_{k-q}; total +2ε a_{k-q}
Quadratic if p and q both in same term when i=p and k-i=q => p+q=k: term originally 2a_p a_q (if p≠q) or a_p^2 if p=q; new 2(a_p-ε)(a_q+ε) = 2a_pa_q + 2ε(a_p-a_q)-2ε^2. Linear total from above for k=p+q: -2ε a_q + 2ε a_p =2ε(a_p-a_q) correct. Quadratic -2ε^2. Good.
If p=q not allowed.

Now, if p+q out of [0,L-1]? p,q in 0..n-1 so p+q in 0..2n-2 always. Good.

Now, if `a` has zeros, `pair_move_max` with eps=frac*a[p] maybe 0 for p zero; skipped.

Now, final `return [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]`. Need if best_a None? set default.

```
def structured_init(n, mode, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    e=1e-6
    mn=mode % 7
    if mn==0: a=np.cos(np.pi*x*0.5)**2
    elif mn==1: a=np.sin(np.pi*x)**1.5
    elif mn==2: a=np.exp(-4*x*(1-x))
    elif mn==3: a=np.exp(-12*x*x)+np.exp(-12*(x-1)**2)
    elif mn==4: a=1.0+0.7*(x-0.5)
    elif mn==5: a=1.0+0.25*np.cos(4*np.pi*x)+0.2*np.cos(9*np.pi*x)
    else:
       f1=rng.uniform(1,4); f2=rng.uniform(2,6); ph1=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); ph2=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       a=1.0+0.35*np.cos(2*np.pi*f1*x+ph1)+0.25*np.cos(2*np.pi*f2*x+ph2)
    a=np.maximum(a,e)
    return normalize_positive(a)
```

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2 for nonnegative steps.

Algorithm:
- Start from the supplied previous state-of-the-art sequence if available.
- Use exact, incremental pairwise coordinate swaps guided by active convolution
  peaks to make guaranteed local improvements.
- Use a smoothed-max (LogSumExp) continuation method in log-space with
  custom L-BFGS.  The gradient is computed with FFTs.
- Use an entropic self-play / best-response solver to produce different
  basins of attraction and to improve diverse starts.
- Try several structured/interpolated starts and lengths, then finish with
  final coordinate polishing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft

TARGET_UPPER = 1.5027


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)).copy()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def softmax_log(u):
    u = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64))
    n = len(u)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(u))
    e = np.exp(u - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def softmax_logsumexp(z, T):
    z = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64))
    n = len(z)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    m = float(np.max(z))
    if T <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        close = np.abs(z - m) <= (1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(m)) + 1e-300)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(close))
        if cnt == 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(z))
            w[idx] = 1.0
            cnt = 1
        else:
            w[close] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
        return w, m
    z2 = (z - m) / T
    e = np.exp(z2)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w[z == m] = 1.0 / max(1, int(np.count_nonzero(z == m)))
        if not np.any(w > 0):
            w[int(np.argmax(z))] = 1.0
        return w, m
    w = e / s
    return w, m / T + float(np.log(s))


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(L)
    A = rfft(a, size)
    c = irfft(A * A, size)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def conv_ab(a, b):
    a = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    b = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64))
    n = len(a)
    m = len(b)
    L = n + m - 1
    size = next_pow2(L)
    A = rfft(a, size)
    B = rfft(b, size)
    c = irfft(A * B, size)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def cross_weighted(x, w):
    x = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))
    w = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64))
    nx = len(x)
    nw = len(w)
    if nx == 0 or nw == 0:
        return np.ones(nx, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, nx)
    size = next_pow2(nx + nw - 1)
    A = rfft(x, size)
    B = rfft(w[::-1], size)
    h = irfft(A * B, size)
    return np.maximum(h[nw - 1::-1][:nx], 0.0)


def score_vec(a):
    a = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    n = len(a)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c) / (s * s))


def lse_value_grad_u(u, T):
    """F_T(u) = T log sum_k exp(c_k(a)/T), a=softmax(u).  Returns value, grad_wrt_u, a, c."""
    n = len(u)
    a = softmax_log(u)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = rfft(a, size)
    c = irfft(A * A, size)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    w, lse = softmax_logsumexp(c, T)
    B = rfft(w[::-1], size)
    h = irfft(A * B, size)
    r = np.maximum(h[L - 1::-1][:n], 0.0)

    g_a = 2.0 * r
    mean_g = float(np.dot(a, g_a))
    g_u = a * (g_a - mean_g)
    g_u -= float(np.mean(g_u))

    val = T * lse
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g_u)):
        g_u = np.zeros_like(g_u)
    return val, g_u, a, c


def lse_lbgfs(u0, T, time_budget, maxiter=10000):
    """Custom L-BFGS on smoothed-max objective in log-space."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    u = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64)).copy()
    u -= float(np.mean(u))
    n = len(u)

    if time_budget <= 1.0:
        a = softmax_log(u)
        c = fast_conv(a)
        return normalize_positive(a), float(2.0 * n * np.max(c)), normalize_positive(a), float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))

    m_hist = 12
    s_list = []
    y_list = []
    rho_list = []

    f, g, a, c = lse_value_grad_u(u, T)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        c = fast_conv(a)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
        return a, sc, a, sc

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    final_a = a.copy()
    final_score = best_score
    best_f = f

    it = 0
    fails = 0

    while it < maxiter and (time.monotonic() - t0) < time_budget:
        it += 1
        mh = len(s_list)
        q = g.copy()
        alphas = np.zeros(mh, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(mh - 1, -1, -1):
            alphas[i] = rho_list[i] * float(np.dot(s_list[i], q))
            q = q - alphas[i] * y_list[i]

        r = q.copy()
        for i in range(mh):
            beta = rho_list[i] * float(np.dot(y_list[i], r))
            r = r + (alphas[i] - beta) * s_list[i]

        d = -r
        d -= float(np.mean(d))
        gd = float(np.dot(g, d))
        if (not np.isfinite(gd)) or gd >= 0.0:
            s_list.clear()
            y_list.clear()
            rho_list.clear()
            d = -g
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            gd = float(np.dot(g, d))

        maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if (not np.isfinite(maxd)) or maxd <= 1e-15:
            break

        # Scale step in log-space so one step changes exponents by O(0.3).
        d = d / maxd * 0.35
        gd = float(np.dot(g, d))
        if (not np.isfinite(gd)) or gd >= 0.0:
            d = -g
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if maxd <= 1e-15:
                break
            d = d / maxd * 0.35
            gd = float(np.dot(g, d))
            if gd >= 0.0:
                break

        step = 1.0
        accepted = False
        for _ in range(18):
            trial = u + step * d
            trial -= float(np.mean(trial))
            f_trial, g_trial, a_trial, c_trial = lse_value_grad_u(trial, T)
            if np.isfinite(f_trial):
                if f_trial <= f + 1e-4 * step * gd + 1e-12:
                    accepted = True
                    break
            step *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            fails += 1
            s_list.clear()
            y_list.clear()
            rho_list.clear()
            if fails > 6:
                break
            continue

        fails = 0
        ds = step * d
        gy = g_trial - g
        curv = float(np.dot(ds, gy))
        if curv > 1e-14:
            s_list.append(ds)
            y_list.append(gy)
            rho_list.append(1.0 / curv)
            if len(s_list) > m_hist:
                s_list.pop(0)
                y_list.pop(0)
                rho_list.pop(0)
        else:
            s_list.clear()
            y_list.clear()
            rho_list.clear()

        u = trial
        f = f_trial
        g = g_trial
        a = a_trial
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c_trial))

        final_a = a.copy()
        final_score = sc
        if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
        if f < best_f - 1e-13:
            best_f = f

    final_a = normalize_positive(final_a)
    best_a = normalize_positive(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = final_score
    return final_a, final_score, best_a, best_score


def lse_continue_from(a0, time_budget, levels="long"):
    """Anneal smoothed-max L-BFGS.  Returns best_a, best_score, current_a."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score_vec(best_a)
    current_a = a.copy()

    if time_budget < 5.0 or n == 0:
        return best_a, best_score, current_a

    if levels == "short":
        stages = [1.0, 0.45, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.025]
        reps = 1
        maxit = 5000
    else:
        stages = [1.5, 0.85, 0.5, 0.28, 0.16, 0.09, 0.05, 0.028, 0.015, 0.008]
        reps = 2 if time_budget > 250.0 else 1
        maxit = 12000

    if n > 2500:
        stages = stages[:7]
        maxit = min(maxit, 7000)
    if time_budget < 80.0:
        stages = stages[:3]

    n_stages = len(stages)
    total_steps = n_stages * reps
    step_count = 0

    for rep in range(reps):
        start_idx = 0 if rep == 0 else max(0, n_stages - 3)
        for idx in range(start_idx, n_stages):
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            rem = time_budget - elapsed
            if rem < 2.0:
                break
            remaining = max(1, total_steps - step_count)
            stage_time = max(1.0, min(rem, rem / float(remaining) * 1.05))
            if stage_time < 0.5:
                break
            step_count += 1

            rel = stages[idx]
            T = rel / float(max(1, n))
            u = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            u -= float(np.mean(u))

            a_final, s_final, a_best_stage, s_best_stage = lse_lbgfs(
                u, T, stage_time, maxiter=maxit
            )

            if s_best_stage < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = min(best_score, s_best_stage)
                best_a = normalize_positive(a_best_stage)

            # Keep a sensible current point for the next temperature.
            threshold = max(best_score * 1.03, best_score + 1e-7)
            if np.isfinite(s_final) and s_final <= threshold:
                current_a = normalize_positive(a_final)
            elif np.isfinite(s_best_stage) and s_best_stage <= threshold:
                current_a = normalize_positive(a_best_stage)
            else:
                current_a = normalize_positive(best_a)

            # Tiny multiplicative noise can help avoid exact stationary points.
            if time.monotonic() - t0 < time_budget * 0.85:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(101 + 17 * rep + idx)
                noise = rng.normal(scale=0.03, size=n)
                a_try = (1.0 - 1e-6) * current_a + 1e-6 / float(n)
                a_try = a_try * np.exp(noise)
                a_try = normalize_positive(a_try)
                sc_try = score_vec(a_try)
                if sc_try <= best_score * 1.08 + 1e-8:
                    current_a = a_try

    rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 10.0:
        T = 0.004 / float(max(1, n))
        u = np.log(np.maximum(current_a, 1e-300))
        u -= float(np.mean(u))
        a_final, s_final, a_best_stage, s_best_stage = lse_lbgfs(
            u, T, max(1.0, rem * 0.9), maxiter=maxit
        )
        if s_best_stage < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = min(best_score, s_best_stage)
            best_a = normalize_positive(a_best_stage)
        if 0 <= s_final < best_score:
            best_score = s_final
            best_a = normalize_positive(a_final)

    sc_cur = score_vec(current_a)
    if sc_cur < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_score = sc_cur
        best_a = normalize_positive(current_a)

    return best_a, best_score, normalize_positive(current_a)


def entropic_selfplay(a0, time_budget, seed=5):
    """Fast entropic zero-sum best-response alternating for fixed a.
    Produces plausible new starting points and can improve the incumbent."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score_vec(best_a)
    cur_score = best_score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    outer = 0
    block_iters = 42

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_budget:
        outer += 1
        b = a.copy()
        lam = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

        for _ in range(block_iters):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_budget:
                break
            v = conv_ab(a, b)
            vmax = float(np.max(v))
            if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax <= 0:
                break
            lam, _ = softmax_logsumexp(v, 0.06 * vmax)
            r = cross_weighted(a, lam)
            rmax = float(np.max(r))
            if not np.isfinite(rmax) or rmax <= 0:
                break
            b, _ = softmax_logsumexp(-r, 0.06 * rmax)
            b = normalize_positive(b)

        # Evaluate pure best response.
        sc_b = score_vec(b)
        if sc_b < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc_b
            best_a = b.copy()
        if sc_b < cur_score - 1e-12:
            cur_score = sc_b
            a = b.copy()

        # Blend with current point.
        cur_score = score_vec(a)
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_a = a.copy()

        for t in (0.25, 0.55, 0.85, 1.0):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
            cand = normalize_positive(cand)
            sc = score_vec(cand)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = cand.copy()
            if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                cur_score = sc
                a = cand.copy()

        # Occasionally perturb to change basin.
        if outer % 6 == 0:
            for noise_scale in (0.12, 0.06, 0.03):
                noise = rng.normal(scale=noise_scale, size=n)
                cand = (1.0 - 1e-6) * a + 1e-6 / float(n)
                cand = cand * np.exp(noise)
                cand = normalize_positive(cand)
                sc = score_vec(cand)
                if sc <= best_score * (1.0 + 0.05 * noise_scale) + 1e-8:
                    a = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                    break

        if time.monotonic() - t0 < time_budget - 2.0 and outer % 8 == 0:
            print(f"    selfplay outer={outer} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s best={best_score:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def pair_move_max(c, a, p, q, eps):
    n = len(a)
    L = len(c)
    newc = c.copy()
    scale = 2.0 * eps
    newc[p:p + n] -= scale * a
    newc[q:q + n] += scale * a
    s = p + q
    if s < L:
        newc[s] -= scale * eps
    return float(np.max(newc))


def apply_pair_move(c, a, p, q, eps):
    n = len(a)
    L = len(c)
    scale = 2.0 * eps
    c[p:p + n] -= scale * a
    c[q:q + n] += scale * a
    s = p + q
    if s < L:
        c[s] -= scale * eps

    a[p] -= eps
    a[q] += eps
    if a[p] < 0.0:
        a[p] = 0.0
    if a[q] < 0.0:
        a[q] = 0.0
    return c, a


def pair_refine(a0, time_budget, seed=1):
    """Exact local pairwise-swap minimizer.

    Moves small mass from high active-contribution coordinates to low
    active-contribution coordinates and accepts only if the maximum
    convolution coefficient decreases.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0 or time_budget < 1.0:
        return a, score_vec(a)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * M)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve = 0
    scans = 0
    accepted = 0
    frac_list = (0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_budget:
        scans += 1

        # Periodically recompute to remove any accumulated drift.
        if accepted > 0 and accepted % 25 == 0:
            c = fast_conv(a)
            M = float(np.max(c))
            if M < best_score / (2.0 * n) - 1e-15:
                best_score = float(2.0 * n * M)
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()

        stage = min(3, no_improve // 25)
        P_list = (4, 6, 8, 11)
        Q_list = (16, 26, 40, 54)
        K_list = (3, 4, 5, 6)
        P = P_list[stage]
        Q = Q_list[stage]
        K = K_list[stage]

        L = 2 * n - 1
        if K >= L:
            top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-K:]
        else:
            top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

        r = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in top_idx:
            k = int(kk)
            js = max(0, k - n + 1)
            je = min(n - 1, k)
            idx = np.arange(js, je + 1)
            r[idx] += a[k - idx]
        if len(top_idx) > 0:
            r /= float(len(top_idx))

        order = np.argsort(r)[::-1]

        p_list = []
        for idx in order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if a[idx] > 1e-10:
                p_list.append(idx)
            if len(p_list) >= P:
                break

        q_list = []
        q_set = set()
        for idx in order[-Q:]:
            idx = int(idx)
            if idx not in q_set:
                q_set.add(idx)
                q_list.append(idx)

        if len(r) > 0:
            low_cut = float(np.quantile(r, 0.30))
            low_idx = np.where(r <= low_cut)[0]
            if len(low_idx) > 0:
                add_size = min(12, int(len(low_idx)))
                add = rng.choice(low_idx, size=add_size, replace=False)
                for idx in add:
                    idx = int(idx)
                    if idx not in q_set:
                        q_set.add(idx)
                        q_list.append(idx)

        best_mn = None
        best_p = best_q = best_eps = 0

        for p in p_list:
            ap = float(a[p])
            if ap <= 1e-15:
                continue
            for q in q_list:
                if q == p:
                    continue
                for frac in frac_list:
                    eps = frac * ap
                    if eps <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    mn = pair_move_max(c, a, p, q, eps)
                    if mn < M - 1e-15:
                        if best_mn is None or mn < best_mn:
                            best_mn = mn
                            best_p = p
                            best_q = q
                            best_eps = eps

        improved = False
        if best_mn is not None:
            c, a = apply_pair_move(c, a, best_p, best_q, best_eps)
            s = float(np.sum(a))
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
                a = normalize_positive(a)
                c = fast_conv(a)
                M = float(np.max(c))
            else:
                if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                    a /= s
                    c /= (s * s)
                M = float(np.max(c))
            accepted += 1
            improved = True

        if not improved:
            pos_idx = np.where(a > 1e-9)[0]
            if len(pos_idx) > 1:
                low_cut = float(np.quantile(r, 0.22))
                low_idx = np.where(r <= low_cut)[0]
                if len(low_idx) == 0:
                    low_idx = pos_idx
                for _ in range(40):
                    p = int(rng.choice(pos_idx))
                    q = int(rng.choice(low_idx))
                    for frac in (0.03, 0.10, 0.25):
                        eps = frac * float(a[p])
                        if eps <= 0.0:
                            continue
                        mn = pair_move_max(c, a, p, q, eps)
                        if mn < M - 1e-15:
                            c, a = apply_pair_move(c, a, p, q, eps)
                            s = float(np.sum(a))
                            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
                                a = normalize_positive(a)
                                c = fast_conv(a)
                            else:
                                if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                                    a /= s
                                    c /= (s * s)
                            M = float(np.max(c))
                            accepted += 1
                            improved = True
                            break
                    if improved:
                        break

        if improved:
            if M < best_score / (2.0 * n) - 1e-15:
                best_score = float(2.0 * n * M)
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 120:
                a_cur = None
                for noise_scale in (0.18, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02):
                    noise = rng.normal(scale=noise_scale, size=n)
                    a_try = (1.0 - 1e-6) * best_a + 1e-6 / float(n)
                    a_try = a_try * np.exp(noise)
                    a_try = normalize_positive(a_try)
                    sc_try = score_vec(a_try)
                    if sc_try <= best_score * (1.0 + 0.03 * noise_scale) + 1e-8:
                        a_cur = a_try
                        break
                if a_cur is None:
                    a_cur = best_a.copy()
                a = normalize_positive(a_cur)
                c = fast_conv(a)
                M = float(np.max(c))
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = 0

            if no_improve > 900:
                break

        if scans % 250 == 0:
            print(f"    pair_refine {time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s best={best_score:.8f}")

    # Final truth check.
    final_a = normalize_positive(best_a)
    final_score = score_vec(final_a)
    if final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score
        final_a = final_a
    return final_a, best_score


def structured_init(n, mode, rng):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    m = int(mode) % 7
    if m == 0:
        a = np.cos(np.pi * x * 0.5) ** 2
    elif m == 1:
        a = np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 1.5
    elif m == 2:
        a = np.exp(-4.0 * x * (1.0 - x))
    elif m == 3:
        a = np.exp(-12.0 * x * x) + np.exp(-12.0 * (x - 1.0) ** 2)
    elif m == 4:
        a = 1.0 + 0.7 * (x - 0.5)
    elif m == 5:
        a = 1.0 + 0.25 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.20 * np.cos(9.0 * np.pi * x)
    else:
        f1 = rng.uniform(1.0, 4.0)
        f2 = rng.uniform(2.0, 6.0)
        p1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        p2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        a = 1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x + p1) + 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x + p2)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
    return normalize_positive(a)


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = normalize_positive(f)
    old_n = len(f)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    g = np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    return normalize_positive(g)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800.0):
    """Search for a nonnegative step sequence minimizing the target score."""
    t_global0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_global0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 45.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Load previous state-of-the-art if available.
    try:
        p = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64))
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            pn = normalize_positive(p)
            ps = score_vec(pn)
            if np.isfinite(ps):
                best_a = pn
                best_score = ps
                print(f"Parent loaded: n={len(best_a)} score={best_score:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = structured_init(1200, 3, rng)
        best_score = score_vec(best_a)
        print(f"Default start: n={len(best_a)} score={best_score:.8f}")

    def remaining():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    # Phase 1: guaranteed local improvements by pairwise swaps.
    alloc = min(120.0, remaining() * 0.12)
    if alloc > 20.0:
        print(f"Phase 1: pair_refine budget={alloc:.0f}s")
        a_try, sc_try = pair_refine(best_a, alloc, seed=int(seed) + 1)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
            best_score = sc_try
            best_a = normalize_positive(a_try)
            print(f"  improved to {best_score:.8f}")

    # Phase 2: entropic self-play to obtain different basin representatives.
    alloc = min(120.0, remaining() * 0.12)
    if alloc > 30.0:
        print(f"Phase 2: entropic_selfplay budget={alloc:.0f}s")
        a_try, sc_try = entropic_selfplay(best_a, alloc, seed=int(seed) + 7)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
            best_score = sc_try
            best_a = normalize_positive(a_try)
            print(f"  improved to {best_score:.8f}")
        # Keep self-play result only if it is decent; otherwise continue from best.
        a_self_current = a_try

    # Phase 3: main smoothed-max L-BFGS continuation from incumbent.
    alloc = min(480.0, remaining() * 0.35)
    if alloc > 60.0:
        print(f"Phase 3: LSE continuation budget={alloc:.0f}s")
        a_best, sc_best, a_cur = lse_continue_from(best_a, alloc, levels="long")
        if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_score:
            best_score = sc_best
            best_a = normalize_positive(a_best)
            print(f"  improved to {best_score:.8f}")
    else:
        a_cur = best_a

    # Phase 4: pair polish after LSE.
    alloc = min(300.0, remaining() * 0.22)
    if alloc > 30.0:
        print(f"Phase 4: pair_refine budget={alloc:.0f}s")
        a_try, sc_try = pair_refine(best_a, alloc, seed=int(seed) + 11)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
            best_score = sc_try
            best_a = normalize_positive(a_try)
            print(f"  improved to {best_score:.8f}")

    # Phase 5: multi-start exploration if time permits.
    if remaining() > 220.0:
        print(f"Phase 5: multi-start remaining={remaining():.0f}s")
        starts = []
        n_base = len(best_a)

        if n_base >= 20:
            sym = 0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])
            starts.append((normalize_positive(sym), "sym_parent"))

            for pert_id, noise_scale in enumerate((0.12, 0.25, 0.5)):
                noise = rng.normal(scale=noise_scale, size=n_base)
                pert = (1.0 - 1e-6) * best_a + 1e-6 / float(n_base)
                pert = pert * np.exp(noise)
                pert = normalize_positive(pert)
                starts.append((pert, f"pert{pert_id}"))

        for mode in (3, 5, 4, 6, 0):
            starts.append((structured_init(n_base, mode, rng), f"struct{mode}"))

        if remaining() > 500.0 and n_base <= 2200:
            if n_base < 800:
                n_large = 1600
            elif n_base < 2000:
                n_large = 2400
            else:
                n_large = min(3600, n_base + 1200)
            large = interpolate_sequence(best_a, n_large)
            starts.append((large, f"large_{n_large}"))

        if a_self_current is not None and len(a_self_current) == len(best_a):
            starts.append((normalize_positive(a_self_current), "selfplay_current"))

        # Remove duplicates by identical length/value hash is overkill; limit count.
        if remaining() > 900.0:
            max_starts = min(len(starts), 7)
        elif remaining() > 500.0:
            max_starts = min(len(starts), 5)
        else:
            max_starts = min(len(starts), 3)

        final_reserve = 150.0
        usable = max(60.0, remaining() - final_reserve)
        per_start = usable / max(1, max_starts)

        for idx, (start_a, label) in enumerate(starts[:max_starts]):
            if remaining() < 70.0:
                break
            t_alloc = min(per_start, remaining() - 20.0)
            if t_alloc < 35.0:
                continue

            print(f"  start {label} n={len(start_a)} budget={t_alloc:.0f}s")
            lse_alloc = t_alloc * 0.65
            pair_alloc = t_alloc * 0.30

            a_best_start, sc_best_start, _ = lse_continue_from(
                start_a, max(5.0, lse_alloc), levels="short"
            )
            # It is better to pair-polish the best found in this start.
            pol_a = normalize_positive(a_best_start)

            if remaining() > 25.0:
                p_alloc = min(pair_alloc, remaining() - 12.0)
                if p_alloc > 12.0:
                    a_pol, sc_pol = pair_refine(pol_a, p_alloc, seed=int(seed) + 100 + idx)
                    sc_best_start = min(sc_best_start, sc_pol)
                    if sc_pol < score_vec(pol_a):
                        pol_a = a_pol

            if np.isfinite(sc_best_start) and sc_best_start < best_score:
                best_score = sc_best_start
                best_a = normalize_positive(pol_a)
                print(f"    new best {best_score:.8f} from {label}")

    # Final polish.
    final_reserve = 25.0
    if remaining() > 60.0:
        alloc = min(400.0, remaining() - final_reserve)
        if alloc > 30.0:
            print(f"Final polish budget={alloc:.0f}s")
            a_try, sc_try = pair_refine(best_a, alloc, seed=int(seed) + 999)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
                best_score = sc_try
                best_a = normalize_positive(a_try)

    # If there is still a little time, one more quick smoothed-max pass.
    if remaining() > 90.0:
        alloc = min(70.0, remaining() - 30.0)
        if alloc > 20.0:
            a_best, sc_best, _ = lse_continue_from(best_a, alloc, levels="short")
            if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_score:
                best_score = sc_best
                best_a = normalize_positive(a_best)

    best_a = normalize_positive(best_a)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    best_score = score_vec(best_a)

    # Optional validation against official evaluator if available.
    try:
        official = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
        if np.isfinite(official) and official < best_score:
            best_score = official
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"FINAL returned score: {best_score:.8f} n={len(best_a)}")
    return [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
```
